Kids' Events Digest — Sun Oct 25 → Sat Oct 31, 2026 (ages under 7)

About this digest
  • Next update: Fri Oct 30 (every 5 days, delivered as soon as compiled that morning).
  • Window: Sun Oct 25, 2026 through Sat Oct 31, 2026 — 7 days, America/Los_Angeles. Swept Sun Oct 25, 2026 (PT), with a live-browser verification pass the same day.
  • Home base: ZIP 94560 (Newark, CA). Distances: est. driving miles from 94560, taken from this run's sweep parts; '?' where no prior figure exists.
  • Coverage: baby/toddler/preschool storytimes (ages 0–6) plus all-ages kids/family events. 780 library events in the day lists below (Sun 10/25: 41 · Mon 10/26: 88 · Tue 10/27: 158 · Wed 10/28: 169 · Thu 10/29: 143 · Fri 10/30: 74 · Sat 10/31: 107) plus 94 Beyond listings; 874 total listings. School-age-only, teen, adult, reading-to-dog, and canceled events excluded; 2 virtual feed items dropped (prior digests carry no virtual events).
  • Verification: V = confirmed live this run (feed or browser pass) — library totals: 780 V, 0 U. Beyond: 94 listings (76 V, 18 U). Feed systems reconciled per day (feed total − excluded = kept) for every feed system; the live-browser pass pinned 94 library events across 9 sources (Santa Clara City 6, Livermore 8, Menlo Park 7, Redwood City 15, South San Francisco 10, San Leandro 6, Daly City 7, Berkeley 14, Santa Cruz 21) plus 2 Menlo Park pool events moved to Beyond. San Bruno feed caveat: its CivicPlus feed holds only 21 items total, 13 in-window, so reconciliation is against feed totals, not full-calendar totals. Livermore browser scope: Children's Events calendar only. Tap the V or U to open the event's page.
  • Flags: "free ticket" = pick up at the Children's desk before the program (Hayward rule — see Closures: free ticket 30 min before, every adult AND child, no advance reservations). "borderline" = skews school-age but includes an under-7 age group. "time not shown on listing" = the source page showed no reliable start time (never invented). All-day events are in Activities with "all day" wording so the renderer buckets them separately.
  • All library events are free unless noted. Non-library sources (bookstores, parks, museums, zoos, nature, art studios, Eventbrite, parent calendars) are mostly PAID — costs shown distinctly (PAID/FREE) in Beyond.
  • Layout: each day is split into Storytimes vs Activities (nearest-first within each). A Nearest | Earliest | Latest toggle re-sorts every list; all-day events go in the All-day bucket.
  • Heads-ups: Hayward: free tickets from the Children's desk 30 min before every storytime, for every adult AND child; Hayward is closed Sun–Mon, so no Hayward events Sun/Mon.
